<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> choi</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  username: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'choi'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">choi, password: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'**_123_**'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//{username: "choi", password: "**_123_**"}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Rest</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">one</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">two</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">others</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { one: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, two: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, three: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, four: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, five: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(one, two, others); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1 2 {three: 3, four: 4, five: 5}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  NAME: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  age: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">36</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  password: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'**123'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const 변수를 새로만들듯이 새로운 변수를 세팅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> rename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">NAME</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ name, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">rest });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">rename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(user1));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Promise.prototype.finally()</li>
</ul>
<p>For then, catch, and finally: previously a Promise only had then and catch, but now finally has been added as well.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// resolve, reject이 호출되더라도 finally를 무조건 한번은 타도록 되어있음</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> p1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(resolve, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'before finally'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">})</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">finally</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'call finally'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Asynchronous iteration</li>
</ul>
<p>It became possible to iterate over asynchronous iterable objects.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> req</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> requests) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(req);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es2017es8">ES2017(ES8)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2017es8"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>String padding</li>
</ul>
<p>A method that, for a string shorter than the maximum length, repeatedly fills the padding with a specified string.
padStart pads on the left side of the string, and padEnd does the opposite.
Neither method has any effect on strings longer than maxLength.</p>
<p>String.prototype.padStart(maxLength[, padString])
String.prototype.padEnd(maxLength[, padString])</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// " abc" (두번째 파라미터 생략시 빈 문자열로 채운다)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'12'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "1212121abc"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'1234567'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "12abc"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abcde'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'12'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "abcde"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padEnd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "abc " (두번째 파라미터 생략시 빈 문자열로 채운다)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padEnd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'12'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "abc1212121"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padEnd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'1234567'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "abc12"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'abcde'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">padEnd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'12'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "abcde"</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Object.values / Object.entries</li>
</ul>
<p>Object.values(object)
Object.entries(obj)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> obj</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { a: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, b: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, c: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">keys</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// [ "a", "b", "c" ]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">values</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// [ 1, 2, 3 ]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">entries</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// [ ["a", 1], ["b", 2], ["c", 3] ]</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors</li>
</ul>
<p>getOwnPropertyDescriptor is a method that takes an object and a property name as arguments and returns the property descriptor of that property.</p>
<p>Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(obj, prop)</p>
<p>getOwnPropertyDescriptors takes only an object, without a property name, and returns the property descriptors for all properties within the object.</p>
<p>Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(obj)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> obj1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Jhon'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, age: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">24</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getOwnPropertyDescriptor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj1, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'name'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Object {value: "Jhon", writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getOwnPropertyDescriptors</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(obj1));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Object {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// name: {value: "Jhon", writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true},</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// age: {value: 24, writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// }</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Trailing commas</li>
</ul>
<p>You can now add a comma after the last parameter and argument of a function.</p>
<p>const foo = (a, b, c,) => {}</p>
<ul>
<li>async/await</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// promise를 사용하게 되면 사용자가 얻고자하는 값이 여러개 일경우 then/then/then을 호출하게 되어서 코드가 복잡해진다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getMoviesPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">())</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(result))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getMoviesAsync</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://yts.lt/api/v2/list_movies.json'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getMoviesAsync</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Async Awaite(try catch finally)</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> getMoviesAsync</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  try</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://yts.lt/api/v2/list_movies.json'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(json);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (e) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`Error ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<h2 id="es2016es7">ES2016(ES7)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2016es7"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>Array.prototype.includes</li>
</ul>
<p>The built-in array function includes was added.
It returns a boolean value indicating whether an item exists.</p>
<p>Array.prototype.includes(searchElement, ?fromindex);</p>

<ul>
<li>Exponentiation operator</li>
</ul>
<p>It introduced syntax that is commonly used in other programming languages.
x ** y means x raised to the power of y, which is completely equivalent to Math.pow(x, y).</p>
<p>number ** number</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// before ES2016</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">pow</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// true</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// after ES2016</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 8 ( === 2 _ 2 _ 2 )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">**=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(a); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 81 ( === a _ a _ a \* a )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0.1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 6.25</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 15.588457268119896</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 512</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 512</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> **</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) \</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 64</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es2015es6">ES2015(ES6)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2015es6"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>Advantages of let and const</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayHello</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> errorMessage </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '"name" parameter should be non empty String.'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    alert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(errorMessage);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hello, '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> name </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(errorMessage); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ReferenceError</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 값 수정</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> foo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'foo'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">foo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'foo2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// OK - 값 수정이 가능하다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> bar</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'bar'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">bar </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'bar2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Type error - bar 변수는 상수이므로 값 수정이 불가능하다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 선언, 초기화</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> baz2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Type error - const로 선언한 변수는 선언과 동시에 초기화 해야한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> baz; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// OK - let으로 선언한 변수는 선언과 동시에 초기화할 필요 없다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">baz </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'baz'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const 변수의 프로퍼티 값 수정</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> foo2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">bar2: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'bar'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">foo2.bar2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'bar2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// OK - foo2의 프로퍼티는 수정이 가능하다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">```</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> * Arrow function</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">```</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">javascript</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sum</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Class</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Instroduction classes</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> MakeUser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  userName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  sayHello</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`hello, this is ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">userName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// class와 그냥 객체의 차이점은 new로 할당할 경우에만 instance를 생성한다는 것이다.(MakeUser를 객체를 만들어서 리턴한 것이다.)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.userName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> name;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  sayHello</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`hello, this is ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">userName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'seungah'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> MakeUser;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iPhone</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '아이폰'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iPad</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '아이패드'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iMac</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '아이맥'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> appleProducts</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { iPhone, iPad, iMac };</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayHello1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">aName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'hello '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> aName;</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// after es2015</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayHello2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">aName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> `hello ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">aName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">sayHello2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`hello ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">prev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> prev </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> next;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`prev plus next : ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
