<ul>
<li>Setting default values for function parameters</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'World'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`Hello, ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}!`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">sayName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "Hello, World!"</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayHello</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">aName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hello '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> aName;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">sayHi</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Before ES6, you had to check whether aName was undefined and then set the value again, but now it is possible to set an initial value for an argument.
e.g.) let defalutName = aName || "hong"</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Rest parameters, Spread expression</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Spread</p>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Spread object/Array unpack</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> alpha</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'a'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'b'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'c'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">number, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">alpha]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//[1, 2, 3, 4, "a", "b", "c"]</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Rest</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> bestfriends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">one</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">friendsRest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends : ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">one</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, friends rest : ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friendsRest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">bestfriends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'kim'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'choi'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'seyoung'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Rest &#x26; Spread Destructure</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// object 삭제 &#x26; 정리할경우 유용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  age: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">36</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  password: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'**123'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ignorepwd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">password</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> rest;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">ignorepwd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(user));</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// object를 만들어서 return 할 경우 () 감싸줘야한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setCountry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">country</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'kr'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ country, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">rest }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ({country, ...rest}) spread 문법사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setCountry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(user));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Generator</li>
</ul>
<p>You can pause the flow of a function at a specific point and then resume execution later.
You can emit result values multiple times.
The emergence of the Generator syntax led to the appearance of various libraries such as redux-saga and rxjs.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> gen</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> g</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> gen</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 4</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// undefined</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// next를 수행하면서 각각 여러 동작들을 순서에 맞게 처리가능</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> listPeople</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1. 동작</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2. 동작</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'kim'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3. 동작</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'choi'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 4. 동작</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'park'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> friendTeller</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friends) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friend;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> friends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'kim'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'choi'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'park'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> friendLooper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> friendTeller</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> listG</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> listPeople</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">listG.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//{value: "hong", done: false}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">listG.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//{value: "kim", done: false}</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.race f1,f2,f3중 가장먼저 resolve, reject 되는 내용의 결과값을 제공해준다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> frace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">race</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([f1, f2, f3]);</span></span>

<ul>
<li>Module (ES Module)</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Named export</li>
</ul>
<p>A named export can be done multiple times in a single file. Things exported via named export must later be imported in other modules under the same name they were exported with.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> student</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Park'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> student2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Ted'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> student3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Abby'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {student3};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {student, student2, student3} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'students.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {student </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> park, student2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ted, student3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> abby} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'students.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> \</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> students </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'students.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Default export</li>
</ul>
<p>A default export, on the other hand, can be used only once per script file. And because only an expression is allowed after export default, keywords such as var, let, and const cannot be used.</p>
<p>The objects exported in this way can be accessed by modules. So from now on, let's look at the import statement that brings in objects exported from a module.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Jack'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> jack </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'studentJack'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es5-2009">ES5 (2009)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es5-2009"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>Support for array methods such as forEach, map, filter, reduce, some, and every
Support for getters / setters on Objects</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  firstName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fullName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">person.fullName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hong SeungAh'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">var</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> person </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  firstName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">defineProperty</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(person, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'fullName'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>

<h2 id="es3-1999">ES3 (1999)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es3-1999"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>What we commonly refer to as JavaScript.
Support for JavaScript strict mode (performs stricter? syntax checking).
Support for JSON (in the past XML was used, but as JSON gained popularity it came to be supported).</p>
<ul>
<li>HTML Fragments</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// JS6이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> text1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'HELLO HONG'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> body </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> document.body;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> div </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'div'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> h1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'h1'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">h1.innerText </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> text1;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">div.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">appendChild</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(h1);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">body.innerHTML </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> div.innerHTML;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// JS6이후</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> text1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'HELLO HONG'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> body </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> document.body;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> div </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> `</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  &#x3C;div></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    &#x3C;h1>${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">text1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}&#x3C;/h1></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  &#x3C;/div></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">body.innerHTML </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> div;</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> target</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friendEmails.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">find</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friend.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">includes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'gmail.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>

<ul>
<li>For ... of</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> friends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'kim'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'choi'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'seyoung'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'duhyun'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 잘못된 length의 array에 접근시 undefined가 나오는 문제는 생긴다.(ex: i&#x3C;20)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friends.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> myFriends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friends.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">forEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(myFriends);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const, let 선언 가능(forEach에 비해서)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friends) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Helloo this is string'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
